TL;DR: 本研究探讨了将三维视觉-语言基础模型（VoxTell）用于文本引导的脑肿瘤分割细化。在内部测试集上，正确的指令提示将胶质瘤亚区分割的Dice系数从0.774提升至0.796，优于空白提示和矛盾提示；跨数据集测试中也显示出一致性改进，表明该方法可作为临床医生参与的交互式分割工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的胶质瘤亚区分割对放疗计划和纵向监测至关重要，但手动轮廓修正耗时。现有模型如nnU-Net泛化能力有限，且缺乏临床医生导向的文本修正能力。

Method: 开发了轻量级VoxTell框架：预训练的VoxTell生成初始掩膜；从分割误差中推导Oracle提示，编码目标、动作、位置、影像证据、编辑规模及保留约束；冻结Qwen/VoxTell提示嵌入，通过可训练投影注入多尺度解码器进行条件化，其余权重保持冻结。训练/验证/测试分别使用901/100/250例BraTS-GLI数据，并在100例跨数据集（脑膜瘤、转移瘤、儿童肿瘤、UPENN-GBM）上评估。

Result: 内部测试集（对比增强T1加权输入）上，正确指令将亚区DSC从0.774±0.158提升至0.796±0.137，显著优于空白提示（0.762±0.155）和矛盾提示（0.770±0.163）。跨数据集测试中，正确指令将DSC从0.527±0.287提升至0.550±0.278，优于矛盾提示（p<0.001）。

Conclusion: 三维视觉-语言基础模型能够实现指令引导的胶质瘤亚区分割细化。对正确、空白和矛盾提示的不同响应表明其文本依赖的轮廓编辑能力而非非特异性后处理，支持其作为临床医生参与循环工具的进一步评估。

Abstract: Background: Accurate glioma subregion delineation is important for radiotherapy planning and longitudinal monitoring, but manual contour correction is time-consuming. Models such as nnU-Net may generalize imperfectly and lack clinician-directed text correction.
  Purpose: We investigated adapting a three-dimensional (3D) vision-language foundation model for text-guided brain tumor segmentation refinement.
  Methods: We developed a lightweight VoxTell-based framework. Pretrained VoxTell generated initial masks. Oracle prompts derived from segmentation errors encoded target, action, location, imaging evidence, edit size, and preservation constraints. Frozen Qwen/VoxTell prompt embeddings were injected through trainable projections into its multiscale decoder conditioning; other weights remained frozen. Training, validation, and testing used 901, 100, and 250 BraTS-GLI cases. Cross-dataset transfer was evaluated on 100 meningioma, metastasis, pediatric tumor, and UPENN-GBM cases.
  Results: On the internal test set using post-contrast T1-weighted input, correct instructions improved subregion Dice similarity coefficient (DSC; enhancing tumor, edema, and necrotic/non-enhancing core) from $0.774\pm0.158$ to $0.796\pm0.137$. They outperformed blank prompts ($0.762\pm0.155$; Holm-adjusted $p<0.001$, $d_z=0.71$) and contradictory prompts ($0.770\pm0.163$; $p<0.001$, $d_z=0.48$). In cross-dataset testing, correct instructions improved DSC from $0.527\pm0.287$ to $0.550\pm0.278$ and outperformed contradictory instructions ($0.504\pm0.275$; $p<0.001$, $d_z=0.43$).
  Conclusion: A 3D vision-language foundation model can perform instruction-guided refinement of glioma subregion segmentations. Sensitivity to correct, blank, and contradictory prompts suggests text-dependent contour editing rather than nonspecific post-processing, supporting further evaluation as a clinician-in-the-loop tool.

</details>


### [12] [Adapting Vision Foundation Models with Cascaded Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05393)
*Xi Xiao,Xingjian Li,Cheng Han,Tianyang Wang,Lin Zhao,Yunbei Zhang,Guosheng Hu,Runmin Jiang,Xi Li,Xiao Wang,Min Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种级联语义提示调优方法，通过注入互补的图像先验（手工特征：颜色、纹理、形状）和自注意力图语义先验，在仅调整ViT 0.74%参数的情况下，在34个分类数据集上取得优于现有视觉提示调优方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉提示调优（VPT）方法中，视觉提示是随机初始化的，未利用类似NLP中指令那样的先验知识，这限制了下游任务的适应性能。

Method: 提出两种互补语义先验注入VPT：1）通过经典手工算子提取输入空间的底层图像先验（颜色、纹理、形状）；2）通过自注意力图提供特征空间中的实例感知语义；并设计级联方案在ViT适应过程中整合两种先验。

Result: 在34个具有挑战性的图像分类数据集上，仅调整ViT 0.74%的参数即取得了优于现有方法的下游适应性能。

Conclusion: 向视觉提示注入语义先验是有效的，级联方案能显著提升VPT在下游图像分类任务上的表现，同时保持参数高效的特性。

Abstract: Prompt tuning, a leading parameter-efficient adaptation paradigm in NLP, has recently been extended to computer vision. Visual prompt tuning (VPT) adapts pre-trained vision transformers (ViTs) by updating a small set of additional prompt parameters. However, existing visual prompts are randomly initialized and do not exploit prior knowledge, such as instructions in NLP. We address this gap by injecting two complementary semantic priors into VPT. Fundamental image priors, including color, texture, and shape, are extracted with classical hand-crafted operators and injected into the input space, while self-attention maps provide instance-aware semantics in the feature space. We further propose a cascaded scheme that integrates both priors throughout ViT adaptation. Experiments on 34 challenging image classification datasets demonstrate superior downstream adaptation while tuning only 0.74% of ViT parameters. Project page: https://xixiaouab.github.io/Cascaded-Semantics/.

</details>


### [13] [Invisible Shortcuts: Why Vision Encoders Know Your Camera](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05424)
*Vladan Stojnić,Ryan Ramos,Giorgos Kordopatis-Zilos,Noa Garcia,Giorgos Tolias*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了深度视觉模型中的一种新型捷径学习：像素级的不可见元数据痕迹（如图像处理、摄影采集信息）会被模型作为预测特征利用。大规模的语义监督预训练（如ImageNet标签或LAION图文对）会自然诱导元数据与语义之间的相关性，使模型将低层信号转化为预测特征。实验表明，更强的元数据-语义相关性导致模型对元数据痕迹更高的敏感性和在元数据分布偏移下更大的性能退化。作者还提出了预训练中和预训练后的缓解策略，不仅能降低对目标元数据的敏感性，还能泛化到未见的元数据，且不牺牲下游任务性能。此外，元数据敏感性还解释了部分编码器强大的生成图像检测能力，缓解该敏感性可提升OOD泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究关注可见的偏见（如物体-背景或纹理相关性），但忽略了不可见的像素级元数据痕迹作为捷径学习的来源。作者假设大规模语义预训练会自然诱导元数据-语义相关性，导致模型将低层信号转化为预测特征，这需要被揭示和验证。

Method: 通过引入受控的元数据-语义相关性实验来验证假设，系统性地评估模型对元数据痕迹的敏感性及分布偏移下的性能退化，并提出预训练中和预训练后的缓解策略来降低敏感性。

Result: 更强的元数据-语义相关性导致更高的元数据敏感性和更大的分布偏移性能退化；所提出的缓解策略能降低对目标及未见元数据的敏感性且不影响下游任务性能；元数据敏感性部分解释了强生成图像检测能力，缓解后可提升OOD泛化。

Conclusion: 不可见元数据痕迹是深度视觉模型捷径学习的重要来源，需要在预训练和微调阶段加以关注和缓解。该发现对理解模型泛化机制、提升OOD鲁棒性以及生成图像检测具有重要启示。

Abstract: Deep vision models exploit shortcuts, relying on cues that correlate with supervision signals. Prior work has focused on visible biases, such as object-background or texture correlations. We identify a different source of shortcut learning: invisible metadata traces embedded at the pixel level, for metadata such as image processing and photo acquisition. We hypothesize that large-scale semantic supervision, whether through categorical labels (ImageNet) or billion-scale captions (LAION), naturally induces metadata-semantics correlations during pretraining, leading models to convert low-level signals into predictive features. By introducing controlled metadata-semantics correlations, we show that stronger ones produce systematically higher sensitivity to metadata traces and larger performance degradation under metadata distribution shifts. We further explore mitigation strategies applied during and after pretraining that reduce sensitivity not only to targeted metadata but also to unseen ones, without sacrificing performance on downstream tasks. Metadata sensitivity also has a positive side: it partly explains the strong generated-image detection ability of some encoders, while its mitigation can improve out-of-distribution generalization. Code: https://github.com/ryan-caesar-ramos/visual-encoder-traces

</details>


### [14] [MOSAIK: Multi-Patch Content-Aware Spatial Allocation of Image Tokens for Efficient Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05450)
*Mohammadreza Hami,Mohammadreza Samadi,Chao Gao,Negar Hassanpour*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MOSAIK是一个基于"损伤引导"的像素空间扩散模型效率优化框架，通过在不同区域和去噪步骤间自适应调整patch大小来降低计算成本。在减少70% FLOPs和83% token数量的情况下，MOSAIK在GenEval上与全计算PixelDiT持平，DPG-Bench仅下降1.0分，并在高约束计算场景下优于现有效率方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素空间扩散模型在图像空间直接生成，避免了隐空间扩散模型的重建天花板，但其自注意力的二次复杂度导致生成成本高昂。现有效率方法在同一去噪步骤中全局使用单一patch大小，忽略了图像不同区域在粗化时面临的保真度损失差异，缺乏区域自适应的粒度控制。

Method: MOSAIK提出损伤引导的框架：1) 将PixelDiT骨干网络改造以支持生成任意的异构patch布局；2) 设计轻量级预测器，利用中间去噪特征估计每个区域因粗化导致的保真度损失；3) 给定token预算，将细patch分配给敏感区域，粗patch用于其他区域，实现跨区域和跨步骤的动态patch大小调度。

Result: MOSAIK在减少70% FLOPs和83% token数量后，GenEval基准上与全计算PixelDiT表现持平，DPG-Bench得分仅下降1.0分。与时间patch调度和特征缓存等效率范式相比，MOSAIK在中度预算下具有高度竞争力，并在高度受限的计算场景中持续优于这些基线。

Conclusion: 通过损伤感知的自适应patch布局，MOSAIK在像素空间扩散模型中实现了显著的计算效率提升，同时保持了生成质量，证明区域粒度差异化是超越时间维度统一调度的有效效率优化方向。

Abstract: Pixel-space diffusion models avoid the reconstruction ceiling of latent diffusion models by generating directly in image space. However, their substantially higher token count makes generation expensive due to the quadratic complexity of self-attention. Several existing efficiency methods reduce this cost by using larger patches at selected denoising steps, thereby representing the image with fewer tokens. Yet, each step still uses a single patch size uniformly across the entire image, overlooking that different regions suffer different fidelity losses when coarsened. We introduce MOSAIK, a damage-guided framework that varies patch size across regions and denoising steps. MOSAIK adapts the PixelDiT backbone to generate arbitrary heterogeneous patch layouts, and a lightweight predictor uses intermediate denoising features to estimate the fidelity loss caused by coarsening each region. Given a token budget, our damage-guided layout predictor assigns fine patches to sensitive regions and coarse patches elsewhere. Remarkably, while reducing FLOPs by 70% and token count by 83%, MOSAIK matches the full-compute PixelDiT on GenEval and its DPG-Bench score drops by only 1.0 point. Compared to diverse efficiency paradigms, including temporal patch scheduling and feature caching, our approach delivers highly competitive performance at moderate budgets and consistently outperforms these baselines in highly constrained compute regimes.

</details>


### [15] [CDSeg: A Renderable Gaussian Carrier for Image-to-3D Label Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05482)
*Wentao Sun,Yiping Chen,Zhengsen Xu,Jonathan Li,John S. Zelek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CDSeg是一个基于高斯泼溅的标签转移接口，无需任务特定的3D分割训练，利用外部2D掩码源和渲染器可见性将像素标签关联到3D高斯原语上，支持点云、高斯场景和多视图图像之间的跨域标签复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代图像模型能很好地判断每个视图中应分割什么内容，但其输出掩码无法直接确定这些标签在3D空间中的具体位置。现有方法通常需要针对特定任务训练3D分割网络，缺乏一个通用的标签转移接口。

Method: CDSeg将高斯原语作为可渲染的标签载体。外部掩码源提供标签，渲染器推导的可见性决定哪些3D原语接收标签。载体可通过将每个输入点补全为一个高斯（保留索引）或复用优化后的高斯场景原生原语来实例化。在渲染过程中记录像素-原语关联，通过投票和局部滤波器融合多视图掩码。

Result: 在DesktopObjects-360上达到92.35% mIoU，在NeRDS-360上达到95.89%，在ScanNet-v2完整验证集上使用提供的2D语义标注达到65.77% mIoU。可处理数百万原语的场景且只需数秒。

Conclusion: CDSeg提供了一个统一的接口，可在点云、高斯场景和图像视图之间复用2D掩码，无需特定任务的三维分割网络，覆盖可提示、自动实例、语义和LiDAR等多种设置。

Abstract: Modern image models provide strong cues about \emph{what} should be segmented in each view, but their masks do not by themselves determine \emph{where} those labels should persist in 3D. We present Cross-Domain Segmentation via Gaussian Splatting (CDSeg), a label-transfer interface that requires no task-specific 3D segmentation training and uses Gaussian primitives as a renderable label carrier. An external mask source supplies the labels, while renderer-derived visibility determines which 3D primitives receive them. The carrier is instantiated either by completing each input point into one Gaussian, preserving its index, or by reusing the native primitives of an optimized Gaussian scene. CDSeg records pixel--primitive associations during rendering and fuses multi-view masks through voting and a local filter. The resulting labels can be returned to the original points, retained on the native Gaussian scene, or rendered into other views. CDSeg covers promptable, automatic instance, semantic, and LiDAR settings and processes scenes with millions of primitives in seconds. It obtains 92.35\% mIoU on DesktopObjects-360, 95.89\% on NeRDS-360, and 65.77\% on the full ScanNet-v2 validation split using the provided 2D semantic annotations. CDSeg thereby provides one interface for reusing 2D masks across point clouds, Gaussian scenes, and image views without a task-specific 3D segmentation network.

</details>


### [16] [VideoArgus: Agentic Rubric-Grounded Unified Evaluation for Video Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05485)
*Ziyun Zeng,Zixuan Wang,Yongsheng Yu,Hang Hua,Jiebo Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了VideoArgus，一个统一的基于rubric的视频生成与编辑评估框架，涵盖五种生成/编辑设置。每个输入实例生成一次输出无关的样本特定rubric，复用于评估所有候选视频。构建了包含1,026个输入实例的VideoArgus-Bench基准，在1,260个视频的人类对齐集上，五个任务中均在输入内Spearman和Kendall相关系数上优于对应基准专用评估器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成评估基准存在三个问题：依赖固定评估内容、仅覆盖部分生成和编辑设置、分数缺乏充分证据支撑。需要更全面且证据充分的统一评估框架。

Method: VideoArgus对每个输入实例生成一次输出盲的、样本特定的rubric（包含具体标准、评分规则、失败模式和证据计划），并复用该rubric评估所有对应候选视频。通过标准特定的VLM问答和视觉工具，生成有证据支撑的标准分数、理由和诊断报告。建立了包含1,026个输入实例的VideoArgus-Bench，rubric预生成并冻结发布。

Result: 在1,260个视频的人类对齐测试集上，五个任务中VideoArgus的输入内Spearman和Kendall相关系数均优于对应基准专用评估器。模型排名在不同rubric生成和评估VLM骨干下保持基本一致。

Conclusion: VideoArgus作为统一、rubric驱动的视频评估框架，在人类一致性上优于各任务专用评估器，且具备跨骨干模型的鲁棒性，代码和数据已全部开源。

Abstract: Evaluating generated videos remains challenging because existing benchmarks rely on fixed evaluation content, cover only a subset of generation and editing settings, and provide limited evidence for their scores. We introduce VideoArgus, a unified rubric-grounded framework covering five video generation and editing settings. For each input instance, VideoArgus generates an output-blind, sample-specific rubric once and reuses it to evaluate all corresponding candidate videos. The rubric defines concrete criteria, scoring rules, failure modes, and evidence plans, which guide criterion-specific VLM QA and visual tools to produce evidence-grounded criterion scores, rationales, and a diagnostic report. We further construct VideoArgus-Bench, containing 1,026 curated input instances built from 653 high-quality images and 416 high-quality videos, with all benchmark rubrics pre-generated, frozen, and released. On a separate 1,260-video human-alignment set, VideoArgus achieves higher within-input Spearman and Kendall correlations with human judgments than the corresponding benchmark-specific evaluators across all five tasks. Model rankings also remain largely consistent across different rubric-generation and evaluation-VLM backbones. All code and data are released. Visit our project page: https://zzzmyyzeng.github.io/VideoArgus

</details>


### [17] [APQF: Agentic Profiling-Guided Structured Pruning and Mixed-Precision Quantization with Adaptive Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05499)
*Sadegh Jafari,Mohiuddin Bilwal,Fan Zhou,Brian Gelder,Ali Jannesari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: APQF是一个基于代理(agent)引导的自动化压缩框架，结合结构化剪枝、混合精度量化感知训练和精度恢复，通过配置分析智能体度量模型各层的计算成本与敏感性，由LLM规划器提出逐层剪枝比例和位宽，并在多个CNN和视觉Transformer架构上实现13-18倍的计算量缩减，同时保持接近基线的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络规模庞大，在边缘设备上部署成本高、速度慢。现有剪枝和量化方法依赖人工专家经验，难以跨架构通用，且统一的压缩设置忽略了不同层对压缩敏感性的差异，导致精度损失。

Method: APQF采用智能体引导框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 配置分析智能体度量模型各层的计算成本分布和对剪枝的敏感度；(2) LLM规划器基于这些证据提出逐层剪枝比例、逐层位宽和恢复策略；(3) 在一个自动化流水线中整合结构化剪枝、混合精度量化感知训练和精度恢复，且所有决策在执行前都经过验证。

Result: 在ImageNet-1k上，APQF将计算量降至原始比特运算的5.6-7.7%（13-18倍缩减），精度接近基线；在200K图像预算下Top-1比现有联合剪枝量化方法高约17个百分点。在CIFAR-10上五个架构中有四个压缩效果更好。VGG7达到93.15%精度，仅用基线0.41%的比特运算。六个LLM规划器在Swin-Tiny上均达到97.4-97.9%的精度。

Conclusion: APQF是首个将LLM引导的、基于配置分析的决策与完全训练感知的剪枝量化流水线相结合的统一框架。消融实验表明均匀压缩在匹配计算量下精度损失最大，且剥夺规划器的配置分析数据会损害所有模型性能，验证了配置引导和LLM规划的有效性。

Abstract: Modern deep neural networks achieve strong performance, but their scale makes them costly and slow, especially on resource-constrained edge devices. Pruning and quantization address this, but rely on manual, expert choices and on algorithms that are hard to apply across architectures. Uniform settings also ignore how differently individual layers respond to compression, which costs accuracy. We introduce APQF, an agentic profiling-guided framework that combines structured pruning, mixed-precision quantization-aware training, and accuracy recovery in one automated pipeline. A profiling agent measures how cost is distributed across the model and how sensitive each part is to pruning, and this evidence drives per-layer pruning ratios, per-layer bit-widths, and the recovery strategy, all proposed by LLM planners and validated before execution. To our knowledge, APQF is the first framework to combine LLM-guided, profiling-grounded decisions with a fully training-aware pruning and quantization pipeline for both CNNs and vision transformers. We evaluate APQF on ResNet, VGG7, ViT, DeiT, and Swin using ImageNet-1k and CIFAR-10. On ImageNet it cuts compute to 5.6-7.7 percent of the original bit-operations, a 13-18x reduction, while keeping accuracy close to the baseline, and under a 200K-image budget it stays roughly 17 points higher in Top-1 than existing joint pruning and quantization methods. On CIFAR-10 it compresses further than that method on four of five architectures. On VGG7 it reaches 93.15 percent using only 0.41 percent of baseline bit-operations, the only method at that compression level to improve on its full-precision baseline. Ablations show that uniform compression loses the most accuracy at matched compute, and that withholding profiling data from the planner hurts every model. Six LLM planners, including free open-weight ones, all reach 97.4-97.9 percent on Swin-Tiny.

</details>


### [18] [DynaPix: Can Vision-Language Models Identify the Exact Future?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05505)
*Thong Nguyen,Vinh-Hien Do,Quynh Vo,Cong-Duy Nguyen,See-Kiong Ng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了DynaPix基准，用于检验模型在物理场景中预测未来真实状态的能力。通过物理模拟器精确生成未来图像及干扰项，发现模型在事件锚定预测上表现良好，但在纯时间锚定预测上接近随机水平，暴露了"时间锚定差距"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估往往只接受文字或看似合理的图像，预测结果从未与真实状态进行核验。物理场景行动要求知晓真实的后继状态，而非仅是一个合理状态，因此需要一个可检验的预测基准。

Method: 构建DynaPix基准：从物理模拟器中获取视频片段（在关键事件前停止），模型需要从候选图像或大规模图库中选出真实未来图像。正确图像及其时间由模拟器精确已知，错误选项被故意设计为高度相似。同时引入基于模拟器真实记录的场景描述训练方案。

Result: 模型在存在可见事件标记目标时刻时表现良好，但仅靠经过时间标记目标时刻时接近随机水平。图库搜索更难，真实图像很少排名第一。人类在纯时间项目上表现良好，说明困难在模型而非问题。基于模拟器真实记录的训练修复了大部分缺陷，但无法解决较长时间间隔的情况。

Conclusion: DynaPix揭示了时间锚定差距：模型将预测附着于可见事件的能力远强于将预测附着于时间本身的能力。这指出了当前模型在物理场景预测中对纯时间线索的建模不足。

Abstract: Acting in a physical scene requires knowing its real later state, not a plausible one. Current evaluations often accept words or a realistic-looking image, so the predicted state is never checked against the true one. We introduce DynaPix (Dynamic Pixels), a benchmark that makes prediction checkable. Given a video clip that stops before a key event and a question about a later moment, a model must pick the true future image from close candidates or a large gallery. The scenes come from a physics simulator, so the correct image and its time are known exactly and the wrong options are deliberately similar. Models often succeed when a visible event marks the target moment, but are near chance when only elapsed time marks it. Gallery search is harder still, as the true image rarely ranks first. People handle the elapsed-time items well, so the difficulty lies with the models, not the questions. Training on scene accounts drawn from the simulator's true record, not a teacher's guess, repairs much of this but not the longer elapsed time case. DynaPix thus exposes a temporal-anchoring gap: models attach a prediction to an event far better than to time itself.

</details>


### [19] [HERA: Historical Evidence Routing Adapter for Physical Prediction in Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05523)
*Yuanruyi,Yue Cao,Haojia Gao,Guanqiu Guo,Ziyuezhang,Shangqin,Junbo Tan,Bokui Chen,Zhuo Zou,Xueqian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HERA（历史证据路由适配器）框架，通过Register-Routed Patch Memory (RRPM)轻量适配器，将保留的历史证据选择性地路由到冻结的潜在视频预测器中，以解决遮挡条件下物理事件预测的难题。在IntPhys2 Main数据集上，将V-JEPA 2-G的成对AvgSurprise准确率从52.57%提升至54.35%，在固定相机连续性和不变性子任务上分别提升至57.69%和63.46%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测性视频模型作为世界模型在学习潜在视觉动态方面表现良好，但在遮挡条件下的物理事件预测仍面临挑战——后续预测可能依赖当前视图中已不可见的物体证据。现有方法主要通过扩大时间上下文、缓存通用视频特征或施加显式对象中心状态来提升历史保留的容量或结构，但未直接解决如何选择性检索并将相关历史证据集成到预训练预测器中，且不能干扰其原有的潜在工作空间。

Method: HERA框架的核心思想是将保留的历史证据路由到冻结的潜在预测器中。具体实现为RRPM（Register-Routed Patch Memory），这是一个轻量级适配器，由三个组件构成：结构化记忆库（Structured Memory Bank）用于存储历史证据，记忆寄存器（Memory Registers）用于索引和检索相关历史，以及工作空间寄存器（Workspace Registers）用于将检索到的证据集成到预测器的推理过程中，且不干扰其原生的潜在工作空间。

Result: 在IntPhys2 Main数据集上，HERA+RRPM将V-JEPA 2-G的成对AvgSurprise准确率从52.57%提升至54.35%。子组分析显示，在固定相机连续性任务上从46.15%大幅提升至57.69%，在固定相机不变性任务上从46.15%提升至63.46%，均表现出显著改善。

Conclusion: 结果表明，历史证据路由是一种实用的适配策略，能够在不修改预训练潜在世界模型的前提下，有效解决遮挡条件下物理预测对历史证据的需求，为潜在世界模型中的物理预测提供了一种新的适应路径。

Abstract: Predictive video models have emerged as promising world models by learning latent visual dynamics from large-scale video. Yet these models remain challenged by physical events under occlusion, where later predictions may depend on object evidence that is no longer available in the current view. Addressing this challenge requires historical evidence not only to be preserved but also to remain accessible when it becomes relevant to a subsequent prediction. Existing approaches mainly enlarge the temporal context, cache generic video features, or impose explicit object-centric states, thereby improving the capacity or structure of retained history. However, they do not directly address how relevant historical evidence can be selectively retrieved and integrated into a pretrained predictor without interfering with its native latent workspace. Accordingly, we introduce HERA (Historical Evidence Routing Adapter), a framework for routing retained historical evidence into a frozen latent predictor, and instantiate it with Register-Routed Patch Memory (RRPM), a lightweight adapter comprising a Structured Memory Bank, Memory Registers, and Workspace Registers. On the IntPhys2 Main split, HERA with RRPM improves the pairwise AvgSurprise accuracy of V-JEPA 2-G from 52.57% to 54.35%. Subgroup analysis shows particularly strong improvements on fixed-camera continuity, from 46.15% to 57.69%, and fixed-camera immutability, from 46.15% to 63.46%. These results support historical evidence routing as a practical adaptation strategy for physical prediction in latent world models.

</details>


### [20] [OmniMech: All-in-one Multimodal Mechanical Benchmark for 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05539)
*Taiting Lu,Runze Liu,Ziwei Dong,Sisong Bei,Jingying Zeng,Mingjia Wang,Zhenghao Li,Kaiyuan Lin,Yi-Shan Wu,Yangshoudu Zheng,Hongxing Pan,Kai Zhang,Guoliang Shi,Ling Ma,Yifan Yang,Jiaying Lu,Qi He,Sung-Liang Chen,Yi-Chao Chen,Yincheng Jin,Mahanth Gowda*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了OmniMech，这是首个百万级规模的工业机械设计CAD生成基准测试集，包含超25万张带完整尺寸和公差标注的二维工程图纸及其对应的原生CAD模型、多视图渲染、网格、STEP和B-rep表示。基准涵盖四个任务：参数化CAD程序生成、图到3D推理、标注推理和工具增强的智能体推理。实验表明现有VLM和CAD专有模型在这类细粒度工业任务上仍面临重大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型虽然能从图像生成可执行的CAD程序，但主要针对粗粒度、通用型3D物体，缺乏对工业机械设计中细粒度几何和毫米级公差要求的处理能力，因此需要专门的基准来评估和推动该领域发展。

Method: 构建包含251,000+张全尺寸和公差标注的二维正交工程图纸的百万级基准OmniMech，每个样本配对原生CAD模型、多视图渲染、网格、STEP和B-rep表示及丰富语义标注。基准定义了四个评估任务：参数化CAD程序合成、图到3D推理重建、基于标注的尺寸/符号/特征/制造约束推理、以及使用可视化、测量和执行的工具增强智能体推理。

Result: 实验结果表明，当前的VLM和CAD专有模型在执行程序合成、细粒度3D重建以及尺寸和公差的可靠执行方面仍然表现不佳，说明该领域存在显著的性能差距。

Conclusion: OmniMech作为首个工业级CAD生成基准，将公开基准数据、评估代码和工具接口，为未来工业机械设计场景下VLM能力提升提供支撑。

Abstract: Recent vision-language models (VLMs) can generate executable CAD programs from images, but existing methods mainly target coarse, general-purpose 3D objects and rarely address the fine-grained geometry and millimeter-level tolerances required in industrial mechanical design. We introduce OmniMech, the first million-scale benchmark for evaluating VLMs on executable CAD generation from industrial manufacturing data. OmniMech contains more than 251,000 fully dimensioned and toleranced 2D orthographic drawings, paired with native CAD models, multi-view renderings, mesh, STEP and B-rep representations, and rich semantic annotations. The benchmark includes four tasks: (1) parametric CAD program synthesis from engineering drawings; (2) diagram-to-3D reasoning for geometrically and structurally consistent reconstruction; (3) annotation-grounded reasoning over dimensions, symbols, feature callouts, and manufacturing constraints; and (4) tool-augmented agentic reasoning using visualization, measurement, CAD execution, and verification tools. Experiments show that current VLMs and CAD-specialized models still struggle with executable program synthesis, fine-grained 3D reconstruction, and reliable enforcement of dimensions and tolerances. We will release the benchmark data, evaluation code, and tool interfaces to support future research.

</details>


### [21] [Hierarchical Flow Matching for 3D Point Cloud Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05557)
*Linhao Wang,Qichang Zhang,Ye Su,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出层次化流匹配（HFM），将无条件3D点云生成分解为潜空间流匹配和条件点流匹配两个层级，通过最优传输流匹配训练，以简化的MSE回归损失实现高效采样（每流仅需15步Euler），在ShapeNet和ModelNet上达到与SOTA相当或更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D点云生成方法存在两大问题：基于流的CNF方法需要昂贵的ODE求解和迹估计，扩散模型需要数百次迭代去噪；同时大多方法在点空间单层生成，忽视了3D形状固有的层次结构。

Method: 将任务分解为两个层级：Latent Flow Matching在紧凑潜空间中建模全局形状流形，Conditional Point Flow Matching在潜码条件下重建细节点云；两个流均通过最优传输流匹配训练，使用简单的MSE回归损失，产生直线OT路径。

Result: 每个流仅需15个Euler步即可高效采样；潜空间结构支持分类等下游任务；在ShapeNet和ModelNet基准上取得与先前SOTA方法竞争性或最优的性能。

Conclusion: HFM通过层次化双流匹配框架，在保证生成质量的同时大幅提升采样效率，并赋予潜空间结构以支持下游任务，验证了层次化分解在3D点云生成中的有效性。

Abstract: Generating high-quality 3D point clouds requires capturing both global shape topology and local geometric details. Existing flow-based methods rely on continuous normalizing flows (CNFs) that demand expensive ODE solving and trace estimation during training, while diffusion models require hundreds of iterative denoising steps. Moreover, most approaches adopt single-level generation directly in point space, disregarding the hierarchical structure natural to 3D shapes. We propose Hierarchical Flow Matching (HFM) that extends flow matching to bilevel structure for unconditional 3D point cloud generation. HFM decomposes the task into two levels via optimal-transport flow matching: a \textit{Latent Flow Matching} models the global shape manifold in a compact latent space, and a \textit{Conditional Point Flow Matching} reconstructs detailed point clouds conditioned on the latent code. Both flows are trained with simple MSE regression losses. The resulting straight OT paths enable efficient sampling with as few as 15 Euler steps per flow, while the structured latent space supports downstream tasks including classification. Extensive experiments on ShapeNet and ModelNet benchmarks demonstrate that HFM achieves competitive or even best performance compared with prior state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [22] [From Sports to Safety: Benchmarking Proactive Risk Inference in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05560)
*Jiawei Qiu,Yichen Xu,Jianzhe Ma,Mingyang Yu,Wenbin Zhu,Yang Han,Pinzheng Lv,Wenxuan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SPRINT基准测试，包含2,888个真实运动视频，用于评估多模态大语言模型（MLLM）在物理危险预测方面的能力。实验发现当前最先进的MLLM在危险信号检测上虽能达到95%以上准确率，但理解事故原因的能力不足50%，且存在严重的误报问题，表明模型仅具备表面的主动安全能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的MLLM评估主要集中在有害内容或一般风险上，缺乏对主动物理危险预测这一维度的系统性评估。运动场景为测试MLLM在危险预测方面的能力提供了理想测试平台，因为运动事故涵盖多种伤害维度，且事故前的时空线索推理能力与自动驾驶、跌倒检测等更广泛的安全领域共享。

Method: 作者构建了SPRINT基准测试，涵盖14种运动和3种环境场景，共2,888个真实世界运动视频（其中2,440个事故视频，448个安全对照组）。事故视频包含细粒度的早期危险线索、事故发生时间和层级原因标注；安全视频经过人工验证确认无事故，用于诊断提示词引发的误报问题。评估时使用多种提示词和时间窗口对最先进的MLLM进行测试。

Result: 实验结果显示，最好的模型在危险信号提示上超过95%准确率，但在识别事故原因上低于50%。诊断实验进一步表明，明确的危险查询在无危险视频上会引发严重误报。这揭示了当前MLLM在危险敏感性和因果理解之间存在显著差距。

Conclusion: 当前MLLM仅表现出表面的主动安全能力，缺乏稳定且基于原因的早期预警。这一发现强调了在动态物理环境中开发可靠主动安全系统的必要性。

Abstract: Timely anticipation of physical hazards is essential for real-world safety, yet existing MLLM evaluations focus on harmful content or general risks, leaving proactive physical hazard prediction underexplored. Sports provide a well-suited testbed: accident causes span diverse injury dimensions and pre-accident spatiotemporal cues draw on reasoning capabilities shared with broader safety domains such as autonomous driving and fall detection. We introduce SPRINT (Sports Proactive Risk INference Testbed), a benchmark of 2,888 real-world sports videos (2,440 accident, 448 safe controls) spanning 14 sports and 3 environmental settings. Accident videos feature fine-grained annotations of early hazard cues, accident timing, and hierarchical causes; safe videos are manually verified as accident-free and serve to diagnose prompt-induced false alarms. Evaluating state-of-the-art MLLMs under diverse prompts and temporal windows reveals a sharp gap between hazard sensitivity and understanding: the best model exceeds 95% in signaling hazards yet falls below 50% in identifying their causes. Diagnostic experiments further show that explicit danger queries trigger severe false alarms even on hazard-free videos. These findings indicate that current MLLMs exhibit only superficial proactive safety, lacking stable, cause-grounded early warning, and underscore the need for reliable proactive safety in dynamic physical environments. Data and code will be open-sourced upon acceptance.

</details>


### [23] [EffectLearner: World-Aware Object-Effect Reasoning for Real-World Video Object Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05565)
*Feier Wu,Wanke Xia,Xu He,Zilang Zhou,Si Chen,Dongxia Liu,Liyang Chen,Qimeng Wu,Zhengbo Zhang,Wenming Yang,Zhiyong Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EffectLearner，一种结合VLM语义推理与DiT视频擦除的框架，用于高质量视频物体移除，同时处理目标物体及其引发的复杂效果（如阴影、反射等）。并构建了EffectWorld配对数据集和渐进训练课程来提升复杂真实场景下的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频物体移除方法主要通过预定义效果类别和固定数据分布隐式学习物体-效果对应关系，难以泛化到包含组合效果、空间分离或弱相关效果、长尾物理现象及动态演化交互的复杂真实场景。

Method: EffectLearner包含两个核心组件：1）基于VLM的Object-Effect Reasoner，通过结构化效果分析提示对目标高亮视频进行跨模态推理，提取紧凑的效果感知上下文；2）基于DiT的Video Eraser，利用推理结果引导全面的物体-效果移除。此外引入运动感知掩码引导和运动一致性监督以提升时空稳定性，并构建EffectWorld配对数据集配合渐进训练课程。

Result: 在标准ROSE-Bench基准上，EffectLearner在大多数指标上优于现有基线；在EffectWorld-Eval和EffectWorld-Wild上取得明显优势，展示了在复杂真实场景中高质量视频物体移除的能力。

Conclusion: EffectLearner通过语义推理增强的方法显著提升了视频物体移除在复杂真实场景下的泛化能力和移除质量，验证了VLM推理与生成模型结合的有效性，并为该领域提供了新的数据集和训练范式。

Abstract: Video object removal must eliminate not only the target object but also its induced effects while maintaining high-fidelity and spatiotemporally coherent restoration. Existing methods mainly learn object-effect correspondences implicitly from predefined effect categories and fixed data distributions, limiting their generalization to complex real-world scenes involving compositional effects, spatially detached or weakly correlated effects, long-tail physical phenomena, and dynamically evolving interactions. We propose EffectLearner, a semantic-reasoning-enhanced framework that combines a VLM-based Object-Effect Reasoner with a DiT-based Video Eraser. Guided by a structured effect-analysis prompt, the Reasoner performs cross-modal reasoning over a target-highlighted video and extracts compact effect-aware context, which guides the Video Eraser toward comprehensive object-effect removal. Motion-aware mask guidance and motion-consistency supervision further improve removal coverage and spatiotemporal stability under object motion and evolving scene dynamics. To fully exploit the framework in challenging real-world scenarios, we further construct EffectWorld, a paired video dataset specifically designed for complex object-induced effects, and introduce a progressive training curriculum that combines common supervision with complex-effect data. On the standard ROSE-Bench, EffectLearner outperforms existing baselines on most metrics and achieves clear advantages on both EffectWorld-Eval and the challenging EffectWorld-Wild, demonstrating its ability to deliver high-quality video object removal in complex real-world scenes.

</details>


### [24] [CoordRefer: Coordinate-Aware 3D Visual Grounding from Multiview Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05569)
*Haijie Li,Jiaxin Zhang,Dave Zhenyu Chen,Youyu Chen,Yanmin Wu,Jian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CoordRefer，一个坐标感知框架，将3D视觉定位中的坐标帧选择和边界框回归解耦，以解决坐标相对框模糊性问题，在ScanRefer数据集上取得了显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多视角基于图像的3D视觉定位方法联合优化坐标帧选择和框回归，导致同一边界框在不同坐标帧下有不同的数值表示，产生多个优化目标，最终得到无效的折中预测，降低了定位性能。因此需要将坐标帧选择与框回归解耦。

Method: CoordRefer框架先选择参考帧定义坐标系，然后基于所选坐标系进行3D框预测。采用坐标感知的监督微调来建立坐标帧选择和坐标条件框回归两个阶段，并使用基于3D IoU奖励的分组相对策略优化（GRPO）来对齐两个阶段与下游定位质量。

Result: 在ScanRefer数据集上配合Qwen3-VL-2B，CoordRefer相比坐标无关的基线在Acc@0.25上提升11%，在Acc@0.5上提升7%。其几何精化变体甚至超越了使用显式3D输入的方法。

Conclusion: 通过将坐标帧选择与坐标条件化接地解耦，CoordRefer有效解决了坐标相对框模糊性问题，显著提升了3D视觉定位性能，且仅使用多视角图像输入即可超越依赖显式3D输入的方法。

Abstract: Multiview image-based 3D visual grounding predicts a coordinate frame to define a coordinate system and then regresses a 3D bounding box for localization. However, existing methods jointly optimize coordinate frame selection and box regression, leading to coordinate-relative box ambiguity and degraded grounding performance. This ambiguity arises because the same box admits different numerical representations across coordinate frames, creating multiple optimization targets and yielding invalid compromise predictions. To tackle this challenge, we propose CoordRefer, a coordinate-aware framework that decouples coordinate frame selection from coordinate-conditioned grounding. CoordRefer first selects a reference frame to define the coordinate system and then conditions 3D box prediction on the coordinate system. We perform coordinate-aware supervised fine-tuning to establish coordinate frame selection and coordinate-conditioned box regression, followed by Group Relative Policy Optimization with 3D IoU-based rewards to align both stages with downstream grounding quality. On ScanRefer with Qwen3-VL-2B, CoordRefer achieves gains of 11% in Acc@0.25 and 7% in Acc@0.5 over the coordinate-agnostic baseline, while its geometrically refined variant surpasses methods using explicit 3D inputs.

</details>


### [25] [Beyond Frame Selection: Rethinking Long-Video Understanding with MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05592)
*Ziling Huang,Shin'ichi Satoh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VideoRouter，通过将视频构建为粗细粒度的时序层次结构，生成全局和局部两种互补视图，并利用验证引导的路由器协调两者来选择最终答案，在VideoMME上超越现有帧选择方法2.9个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在视频理解中受限于token数量，难以捕捉时间上稀疏的关键证据。现有方法（均匀采样或帧选择）通常只优化时间覆盖范围或局部相关性，难以同时保留全局故事线和细粒度证据。

Method: VideoRouter首先将视频组织为问题无关的时序层次结构，将视频划分为由粗到细的时间连贯片段。层次结构上层节点捕获广泛的剧情上下文和事件进展（全局视图），下层节点保留细粒度局部细节和证据时刻（局部视图）。最后引入验证引导的路由器判断哪个视图更受所选证据支持，并选择最终答案。

Result: 在VideoMME数据集上，基于LLaVA-Video-7B骨干网络，方法相比最先进的帧选择方法提升2.9个百分点。扩展实验验证了验证引导路由器能有效协调全局与局部推理。

Conclusion: 视频理解应被重新思考为协调互补证据视图而非选择单一帧子集的问题，通过粗到细的时序层次结构和验证引导的路由机制，可以同时兼顾全局故事线上下文与细粒度证据恢复。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved strong progress in video understanding, yet it remains challenging because the token limitation makes MLLMs difficult to capture temporally sparse evidence. Existing methods typically rely on uniform sampling, or frame selection, but these strategies usually optimize either broad temporal coverage or local relevance, making it difficult to preserve both global storyline context and fine-grained evidence. We propose VideoRouter(VR) that rethinks long-video understanding as coordinating complementary evidence views rather than selecting a single subset of frames. It first organizes each video into a question-agnostic temporal hierarchy, which partition the video into coarse-to-fine temporally coherent segments. In this hierarchy, upper-level nodes capture broad storyline context and event progression, while lower-level nodes preserve fine-grained local details and evidence-bearing moments. This naturally gives rise to two complementary views: a global view for coverage-oriented reasoning and a local view for detail-oriented evidence recovery. We further introduce a verification-guided router to determine which view is better supported by the selected evidence and select the final answer. We validate the effectiveness of the proposed design through extensive experiments, showing that the verification-guided router effectively coordinates global and local reasoning, and that, on VideoMME, our method outperforms state-of-the-art frame selection methods by 2.9 points, respectively, under the LLaVA-Video-7B backbone. We will release the code.

</details>


### [26] [Uncertainty-Aware World Model for Aerial Image-Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05597)
*Deyi Zhu,Haoyu Fan,Yinan Zhu,Weichen Zhang,Shilin Ma,Xinlei Chen,Yansong Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UA-NWM，一种用于无人机航拍图像目标导航的高效潜世界模型，将轨迹评分建模为条件分布外检测，通过分解预测-目标差异为不确定性可解释和不可解释残差，仅用不可解释残差进行评分，在保持低推理延迟的同时优于现有导航世界模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于世界模型的航拍图像导航方法通常仅依赖一个或少数几个点预测来对候选轨迹进行排名，这在大规模户外环境中不足以应对显著的未来状态不确定性，导致导航性能受限。

Method: 提出不确定性感知导航世界模型（UA-NWM），一种高效的潜世界模型。其核心创新是将轨迹评分转化为条件分布外检测问题：通过不确定性子空间表示多种可能的未来，并将预测与目标的差异分解为不确定性可解释和不可解释两个部分，仅使用不可解释残差进行轨迹评分，从而无需多次未来采样即可实现稳健的轨迹选择。

Result: 大量实验表明，UA-NWM在性能上持续优于现有的导航世界模型，同时保持低推理延迟。真实世界无人机实验进一步验证了其实际应用的可行性。

Conclusion: 通过将不确定性建模与轨迹评分相解耦，UA-NWM有效应对了大规模户外环境中未来状态不确定性的挑战，在准确性和效率之间取得了良好平衡，并已在真实无人机平台上得到验证。

Abstract: Aerial image-goal navigation requires an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) to reach a target location specified by a goal image. Existing world-model-based methods rank candidate trajectories using predicted futures, but typically rely on only one or a few point predictions, which is inadequate for large-scale outdoor environments with substantial future-state uncertainty. To address this limitation, we propose the Uncertainty-Aware Navigation World Model (UA-NWM), an efficient latent world model for aerial image-goal navigation, which formulates trajectory scoring as conditional out-of-distribution detection. UA-NWM represents plausible futures with an uncertainty subspace and decomposes the prediction--goal discrepancy into uncertainty-explainable and unexplainable components. Only the unexplainable residual is used for scoring, enabling robust selection without multiple future samples. Extensive experiments demonstrate that UA-NWM consistently outperforms existing navigation world models while maintaining low inference latency. Real-world UAV experiments further validate its practical applicability. Project page: https://duryi.github.io/UA-NWM-Project-Page

</details>


### [27] [ALTER: Modeling Longitudinal Changes via Regional Differencing for 3D CT Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05615)
*Dongchen Li,Jitao Liang,Wei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ALTER通过全局先验集成、区域代理差分和间隔变化融合三个模块，将历史CT与报告纳入三维CT放射学报告生成流程，实现解剖学定位的时间证据表示，在两个胸部CT数据集上取得最先进结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CT放射学报告生成方法虽能建模图像与文本的细粒度对应关系，但仅聚焦于当前检查，未能充分建模患者个体区域内的时间变化；且间隔变化常分布于多个解剖区域，难以对整体纵向状态进行连贯评估。

Method: 提出ALTER框架，包含三个核心模块：(1) 全局先验集成(GPI)将先前CT和报告纳入以建立历史背景；(2) 区域代理差分(RPD)使每个当前解剖区域从先前体积的单一共享编码中检索历史代理，推导局部间隔证据；(3) 间隔变化融合(ICF)将当前异常状态与区域分布差异结合，转换为感知变化的软提示引导报告生成。

Result: ALTER在RadGenome-ChestCT验证集和CTRG-Chest-548K测试集上的大多数评估指标上达到了最先进水平。

Conclusion: ALTER通过显式建模解剖定位的时间证据，有效解决了3D CT放射学报告中纵向变化建模不足的问题，代码与数据预处理已开源。

Abstract: Computed tomography (CT) is widely used for clinical diagnosis and longitudinal follow-up, yet automatically generating accurate and complete radiology reports from three-dimensional (3D) CT remains challenging. Existing methods improve fine-grained correspondence between images and text by modeling anatomical regions, but remain centered on the current examination. Consequently, patient-specific longitudinal changes within individual regions remain insufficiently modeled. Meanwhile, interval changes are often distributed across multiple anatomical regions, complicating a coherent assessment of the overall longitudinal state. We propose Anatomically Localized Temporal Evidence Representation (ALTER) to address these limitations. Global Prior Integration (GPI) incorporates the prior CT and report to establish historical context for the current examination. Regional Proxy Differencing (RPD) enables each current anatomical region to retrieve a historical proxy from a single shared encoding of the prior volume and to derive localized interval evidence. Interval Change Fusion (ICF) further combines current abnormality states with region-distributed differences, converting their joint representation into change-aware soft prompts that guide report generation. ALTER achieves state-of-the-art results on most evaluation metrics across the RadGenome-ChestCT validation and CTRG-Chest-548K test sets. Code and data preprocessing details are available at https://github.com/peytonkarlie/ALTER/tree/main.

</details>


### [28] [TruthLens: Object Hallucination Detection via Self-Evaluating Truthfulness Scores in LVLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05616)
*Yanqi Wu,Runhe Lai,Xinhua Lu,Qichao Chen,Zhiping Zhou,Jia-Xin Zhuang,Weijiang Yu,Ruixuan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TruthLens是一个基于自评估框架的LVLM幻觉检测方法，通过重新利用罕见的特殊token作为参考标记，在每个object-token位置提取该token的log-probability作为真值分数，并通过MSE目标微调LM头使真实对象分数接近1、幻觉对象接近0，同时用散度约束保持原始生成能力。在Qwen2.5-VL-7B上，MS-COCO的AUROC比之前最佳方法提升超过17%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM中的对象幻觉问题严重阻碍了其可靠部署。关键发现是：真实和幻觉对象token在隐藏表示中可清晰分离，但这种可分离性在语言建模（LM）头处基本丧失，因此需要一种机制让LM头本身能够暴露真值信号。

Method: 1) 将一个很少使用的特殊token重新设定为参考token；2) 对每个object-token位置，提取LM头分配给该特殊token的log-probability，并与预定义常数的差值作为真值分数；3) 使用MSE目标进行微调，使真实对象分数趋近1、幻觉对象趋近0；4) 引入散度约束以保持原有生成能力。无需辅助模型或额外推理成本。

Result: 在多个LVLM上取得最先进性能，尤其是在Qwen2.5-VL-7B上，TruthLens在MS-COCO基准上比之前最佳方法AUROC提升超过17%。尽管只在有限对象类别上训练，但对更大标签空间的benchmark仍能有效泛化。

Conclusion: TruthLens通过重新利用特殊token并训练LM头输出真值信号，实现了无需附加模型、无额外推理成本的幻觉检测，且具有良好的跨标签空间泛化能力，代码已开源。

Abstract: Despite the remarkable progress of large vision language models (LVLMs), object hallucination remains a fundamental challenge that hinders their trustworthy deployment. A key finding motivates our work: real and hallucinated object tokens are clearly separable in hidden representations, yet this separability is largely lost at the language-modeling (LM) head. We propose TruthLens, a self-evaluation framework that teaches the LM head to expose a per-object truthfulness signal without any auxiliary model or additional inference cost. Concretely, a rarely-used special token is repurposed as a reference token. For each object-token position, we extract the log-probability assigned to this special token by the LM head, and define its difference from a predefined constant as the truthfulness score. The model is then fine-tuned with an MSE objective that drives scores toward 1 for real objects and 0 for hallucinated ones, while a divergence constraint preserves the original generation capability. Despite being trained on only a limited set of object categories, TruthLens generalizes effectively to benchmarks with substantially larger label spaces. Extensive experiments across multiple LVLMs demonstrate state-of-the-art performance; notably, on Qwen2.5-VL-7B, TruthLens outperforms the previous best method on MS-COCO by over 17\% in AUROC. Our code is available at https://github.com/wyqstan/TruthLens.

</details>


### [29] [Dual-Output Multi-Exposure HDR Reconstruction via SDR Fusion and Gain Map Inverse Tone Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05626)
*Jinho Kim,Jinwoo Kim,Seon Joo Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DOME-HDR框架，通过LoRA适应的潜在扩散模型合成基础SDR图像，再结合HDR先验引导的增益图网络（HPGM）进行动态范围扩展，联合输出感知平衡的SDR图像和高一致性HDR图像，在多个基准数据集上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多曝光HDR重建需要同时输出高质量的HDR和感知平衡的SDR图像，现有方法难以同时兼顾两者的质量与一致性，需要一种联合优化框架。

Method: 1) 使用LoRA适配的潜在扩散模型从三张括号曝光LDR输入合成基础SDR图像；2) 设计双交叉注意力融合模块，从中等曝光锚定，注入欠曝和过曝图像的互补结构与颜色信息；3) 提出HPGM（HDR先验引导增益图网络），利用合成SDR引导预测空间变化的增益图，实现可靠的动态范围扩展。

Result: 在Kalantari、Tel和Challenge123数据集上，使用全参考和无参考指标评估，DOME-HDR达到最先进的HDR重建质量；消融实验验证了双交叉注意力和SDR引导增益图估计的有效性。

Conclusion: DOME-HDR通过联合生成SDR与HDR的双输出框架，利用扩散模型和增益图逆色调映射的方式，实现了高质量且一致的HDR重建，为多曝光HDR重建任务提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: We propose DOME-HDR, a dual-output multi-exposure HDR reconstruction framework that jointly produces a perceptually balanced SDR image and a consistent HDR image via gain map inverse tone mapping. Given three bracketed LDR inputs, DOME-HDR first synthesizes a base SDR using a LoRA-adapted latent diffusion model. A dual cross-attention fusion module injects complementary structural and color cues from the under- and over-exposed images while anchoring on the mid exposure for stability. The synthesized SDR then guides HPGM, our HDR Prior-guided Gain Map network, to predict a spatially varying gain map for reliable dynamic-range expansion. We evaluate on Kalantari, Tel, and Challenge123 using both full-reference and no-reference metrics, where DOME-HDR achieves state-of-the-art HDR reconstruction quality; ablations further confirm the effectiveness of dual cross-attention and SDR-guided gain map estimation.

</details>


### [30] [SCI-CLIP: Segment-Centric Inference with Reference Memory for Training-Free Open-Vocabulary Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05627)
*Mohamad Zamini,Diksha Shukla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SCI-CLIP是一个无需训练的开放词汇分割推理框架，通过统一的片段（segment）抽象来组织特征交互、空间支持、上下文恢复和检索修正等所有阶段，将冻结的CLIP风格视觉特征转化为空间连贯、上下文感知且与检索兼容的密集预测，在八个基准上取得了更好的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放词汇分割仍缺乏推理层面的抽象，冻结的视觉-语言特征仅在patch级别产生，但密集预测需要一个能同时管理特征交互、空间支持、上下文恢复和检索修正的统一单元。现有方法缺少这种统一的推理抽象。

Method: SCI-CLIP首先在冻结的视觉token上诱导区域一致的交互图（region-consistent interaction graph），然后通过在该图上传播值来重建密集特征，并仅在局部证据不足时选择性加入跨窗口支持。随后利用相同的片段抽象构建和查询离线参考记忆，使示例检索与预测单元对齐。

Result: SCI-CLIP在八个基准上一致提升了密集预测的结构质量、上下文推理的鲁棒性以及基于示例的修正对齐，在开放词汇分割任务上取得了更强的表现。

Conclusion: 通过将同一片段抽象贯穿所有推理阶段，无需任何训练即可将冻结的CLIP特征转化为高质量、上下文感知且支持检索修正的开放词汇密集预测，验证了统一推理抽象的设计原则的有效性。

Abstract: Training-free open-vocabulary segmentation remains limited by a missing inference abstraction. Frozen vision-language features are produced at patch level, yet dense prediction requires a unit that simultaneously governs feature interaction, spatial support, contextual recovery, and retrieval-based correction. We present SCI-CLIP, a segment-centric inference framework built around the principle that the same region abstraction should organize all stages of dense open-vocabulary prediction. SCI-CLIP first induces a region-consistent interaction graph over frozen visual tokens, then reconstructs dense features by propagating values over this graph, augmenting them with selective cross-window support only where local evidence is insufficient. The same segment abstraction is subsequently used to construct and query an offline reference memory, aligning exemplar retrieval with the units on which prediction is made. SCI-CLIP turns frozen CLIP-style features into spatially coherent, context-aware, and retrieval-compatible dense predictions without any training. SCI-CLIP consistently improves the structural quality of dense predictions, the robustness of contextual reasoning, and the alignment of exemplar-based correction, yielding stronger open-vocabulary segmentation across eight benchmarks. Project code is available at: https://github.com/mzamini92/SCICLIP.

</details>


### [31] [ChronoVision: Temporal Reasoning via Latent State Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05631)
*Yifan Shen,Jian Xu,Boyi Li,Yuner Zhang,Tianjiao Yu,Bingxuan Li,Houze Yang,Rushi Wang,Xu Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ChronoVision，一个多模态框架，通过重构视觉头（RWH）预测潜在变换状态、ROI注意力定位模块聚焦关键视觉证据，以及带隐式过程机制和复合奖励的强化学习后训练，解决多模态大语言模型在多步时序视觉推理中的不足。并引入Vbvr-VQA数据集用于时序跟踪评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在被动感知方面表现出色，但在需要多步时间推理的复杂视觉认知任务上表现不佳。这主要源于基于语言的推理存在固有歧义，难以准确描述连续的视觉变化过程。

Method: 1) 监督微调阶段：重构视觉头（Reconstructive Visual Head）预测最终变换状态的潜在表示；ROI注意力定位模块通过语义跨度查询聚焦关键视觉证据。2) 后训练阶段：采用带隐式过程接地机制的强化学习，由复合奖励函数引导，评估结果正确性、潜在过程对齐和无监督视觉聚焦。3) 提出Vbvr-VQA数据集，将视频推理重构为严格的图像排序任务以评估时序跟踪能力。

Result: ChronoVision在Vbvr-VQA上取得SOTA性能，域内准确率74.8%，域外准确率71.6%；在极具挑战性的跨域基准IntPhys2上达到55.0%准确率。

Conclusion: ChronoVision通过将视觉逻辑与潜在图像对齐，有效解决了语言推理歧义导致的多步时序视觉推理退化问题，并在多个基准上证明了其优越性与跨域泛化能力。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models excel at passive perception but struggle with complex visual cognitive tasks requiring multi-step temporal reasoning. This degradation largely stems from the inherent ambiguity of language-based reasoning, which often fails to accurately articulate continuous visual transformations. To address this, we propose ChronoVision, a multimodal framework designed to align visual logic with latent imagery. During supervised fine-tuning, a Reconstructive Visual Head predicts the latent representation of the final transformed state, while an ROI Attention Locating module focuses the model on key visual evidence via semantic span queries. In post-training, we apply reinforcement learning with an implicit process grounding mechanism, guided by a composite reward function that evaluates outcome correctness, latent process alignment, and unsupervised visual focus. Furthermore, we introduce Vbvr-VQA, a novel dataset that evaluates temporal tracking by reformulating video reasoning into a strict image-ordering task. Experiments demonstrate that ChronoVision achieves state-of-the-art performance on Vbvr-VQA with 74.8% in-domain and 71.6% out-of-domain accuracy, alongside a strong 55.0% accuracy on IntPhys2, a highly challenging cross-domain benchmark.

</details>


### [32] [Vorch-IR: Long-Form Unified Multimodal Identity Replacement Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05648)
*Yaole Wang,Xiaoyu Chen,Xin Ma,Yang Ding,Gang Yue,Jingjing Chen,Lin Ma,Yaohui Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Vorch-IR是一个统一的视频身份替换框架，基于LTX2构建，支持单人和双人身份替换以及背景替换，通过指令指定参考图像的角色，无需姿势或遮挡匹配，并提出了自动数据构建管线和时序重叠推理策略以支持长视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频身份替换方法大多只针对单人场景，且需要任务特定的结构控制（如掩码或姿态表示），限制了在多模态编辑系统中的灵活性；多人替换因配对训练数据稀缺而进展受限。

Method: Vorch-IR基于LTX2，联合条件化驱动视频、索引参考图像和文本编辑指令。参考图像无需与驱动视频的姿势、布局或空间配置匹配，其作为主体或背景参考的角色由指令指定。密集视觉条件通过自注意力融合，视觉-语言上下文通过交叉注意力建立语义对应。开发了自动数据构建流程，合成四种编辑设置的配对监督数据，并提出时序重叠推理策略以支持分钟级长视频生成。

Result: 自动化指标和成对人工评估表明，Vorch-IR在多种场景下实现了强大的身份保持、运动保真度和时间连贯性，并可将短片模型扩展至分钟级生成。

Conclusion: Vorch-IR通过统一框架解决了单人和双人身份替换及背景替换问题，消除了对特定结构控制的需求，并有效应对了训练数据稀缺的挑战，展示了在实际多模态编辑系统中的广泛应用潜力。

Abstract: Video identity replacement seeks to transfer the identities of one or more subjects while preserving the motion, expressions, and temporal structure of a driving video. Existing methods largely target single-person settings and often require task-specific structural controls, such as masks or pose representations, limiting their flexibility in general multimodal editing systems. Progress on multi-person replacement is further constrained by the scarcity of paired training data. We present Vorch-IR, a unified framework that supports single- and dual-person identity replacement, with optional background replacement, in a single model. Built on LTX2, Vorch-IR jointly conditions on a driving video, indexed reference images, and a textual editing instruction. The reference images need not match the pose, layout, or spatial configuration of the driving video: their roles as subject or background references are specified through the instruction. Dense visual conditions are fused through self-attention, while a vision-language context establishes semantic correspondence through cross-attention. We further develop an automatic data construction pipeline that synthesizes paired supervision for all four editing settings. Experiments using automatic metrics and pairwise human evaluation demonstrate strong identity preservation, motion fidelity, and temporal coherence across diverse scenarios. A temporal overlapping inference strategy additionally extends the short-clip model to minute-long generation without autoregressive continuation.

</details>


### [33] [Vorch-Streamer: Extending Human Audio-Visual Generation to Real-Time Long-Form Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05663)
*Menglin Han,Yang Ding,Yulei Lu,Haoran Yu,Xin Ma,Junyi Chen,Zhangkai Ni,Lin Ma,Yaohui Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Vorch-Streamer，一种后训练框架，用于将预训练的双向生成模型适配为实时长视频音频-视频流式生成，通过混合教师强制与扩散强制训练因果生成器、长时域自强制蒸馏以及外部语言模型预测语音规划Token，实现27.12 FPS的实时T2AV流式生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时长形式角色音视频生成需要因果连续的合成，但预训练双向模型直接适配存在两个关键难题：一是自回归复用生成块作为上下文会产生暴露偏差，导致长rolled-out过程中误差与视觉漂移累积；二是全局语音话语无法指示因果生成器在仅有限局部音视频上下文时应生成哪部分语音内容。

Method: 构建包含8万个12-21秒角色片段的合成语料库；首先使用混合教师强制与扩散强制训练因果生成器；然后应用长时域自强制结合DMD蒸馏，使模型暴露于自身rollout分布同时保持预训练双向教师的质量；外部语言模型预测25Hz离散语音规划Token，其连续特征条件化音频扩散分支并对齐每个因果块应说的内容。

Result: Vorch-Streamer在有限因果上下文和四步去噪条件下，以27.12 FPS联合生成音频和视频，超过24 FPS实时播放速率，同时在长形式生成中保持有竞争力的音频-嘴唇同步和强身份保持。

Conclusion: Vorch-Streamer通过系统的后训练框架成功解决了双向模型向实时因果流式生成的适配难题，兼顾了生成质量与实时性，为长形式文本到音视频流式生成提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Real-time long-form avatar audio--video generation requires causal, continuous synthesis while maintaining audiovisual synchronization and visual consistency. Adapting a pretrained bidirectional model to this setting presents two key dilemmas. First, autoregressively reusing generated blocks as context creates exposure bias, causing errors and visual drift to accumulate over long rollouts. Second, a global speech utterance does not indicates a causal generator which portion should be spoken next when only limited local audio--video context is available. We present \textbf{Vorch-Streamer}, a post-training framework that addresses these challenges and enables real-time long-form Text-to-Audio-Video (T2AV) streaming. We construct a synthetic corpus of 80K avatar clips spanning 12--21 seconds and first train a causal generator with mixed Teacher Forcing and Diffusion Forcing. We then apply long-horizon Self Forcing with DMD distillation, exposing the model to its own rollout distribution while preserving the quality of the pretrained bidirectional teacher. To explicitly control speech progression, an external language model predicts discrete 25-Hz speech-planning tokens, whose continuous features condition the audio diffusion branch and align each causal block with the content it should speak. With bounded causal context and four-step denoising, Vorch-Streamer jointly generates audio and video from text at 27.12 FPS, exceeding the 24-FPS real-time playback rate while maintaining competitive audio--lip synchronization and strong identity preservation over long-form generation.

</details>


### [34] [Dual-Attention and Adversarial Transfer Networks for Sim-to-Real Cross-Orientation Wireless Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05664)
*Linfeng Du,Kehan Wu,Tong Zhang,Rui Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出S2M-Sense系统，通过物理引导的仿真器从单方向运动合成多方向毫米波训练数据，结合双注意力网络提取方向鲁棒特征，并利用对抗无监督迁移学习弥合仿真与真实数据差距，仅需少量无标签真实样本即可实现95%的动作识别准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波人体动作识别在用户朝向变化时性能显著下降，但收集多朝向标注数据成本高且劳动密集，需要一种无需大量多朝向实测数据的方法。

Method: 1) 构建物理引导的仿真器，从单朝向运动合成多朝向无线训练数据；2) 提出双注意力网络，从双链路多普勒频谱图中提取动作判别性强且朝向鲁棒的表示；3) 引入对抗无监督迁移学习机制，仅用少量无标签目标域样本对齐特征分布。

Result: 仿真与实测数据间平均SSIM达0.84；仅用双链路多朝向仿真数据识别准确率达88.33%，迁移学习后仅需16个无标签实测样本即可提升至95%，均优于现有跨域感知方法。

Conclusion: S2M-Sense证明了利用物理仿真数据配合少量无标签真实数据即可有效解决毫米波动作识别中的朝向变化问题，大幅降低数据采集成本。

Abstract: Millimeter-wave human activity recognition suffers significant performance degradation when the user's orientation changes relative to the sensing system, yet collecting labeled multi-orientation data is labor-intensive and costly. To eliminate the need for exhaustive multi-orientation measured data, we develop a physics-guided simulator that synthesizes orientation-diverse wireless training data from single-orientation motion. Specifically, to suppress orientation-induced feature variations, we propose a dual-attention network that extracts activity-discriminative and orientation-robust representations from dual-link Doppler spectrograms. To bridge the simulation-to-reality gap, we introduce an adversarial unsupervised transfer learning mechanism that aligns feature distributions using only a small number of unlabeled target-domain samples. The S2M-Sense platform shows high fidelity in reproducing real-world signatures, validated against 60.48 GHz mmWave measured data with an average structural similarity index measure (SSIM) of 0.84 between simulated and measured Doppler spectrograms across all 4 activities and 4 orientations. Experimental results show that S2M-Sense achieves 88.33% recognition accuracy using only the dual-link multi-orientation simulated dataset, which improves to 95% after simulation-to-reality transfer learning with as few as 16 unlabeled measured samples. Both cases with and without transfer learning outperform state-of-the-art cross-domain sensing methods.

</details>


### [35] [URNet: A Unified Reparameterized Network for Efficient RGB-D Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05671)
*Guoan Xu,Zhengxue Wang,Yang Xiao,Ligeng Chen,Guangwei Gao,Dongchen Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: URNet是一个统一的RGB-D语义分割网络，通过重参数化策略在单一编码器中同时完成多模态特征提取和跨模态融合，并设计了简洁高效的Pyramid Merging Decoder，在多个基准上实现了最先进的性能和高效推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RGB-D语义分割方法使用双编码器分别处理RGB和深度输入，存在两个问题：一是难以充分捕获深度表征并限制了跨模态交互效果；二是额外的编码器分支带来冗余计算，阻碍了轻量化执行。

Method: 提出URNet（Unified Reparameterized RGB-D Network），核心包括：1）采用重参数化策略压缩网络架构以实现快速推理；2）在Reparameterized Block（RepBlock）中引入Linear Gated Attention（LGA）模块，在不同特征尺度上充分利用RGB和深度互补信息；3）设计简洁有效的通用解码器Pyramid Merging Decoder（PMD）。

Result: 在多个RGB-D分割基准数据集上的大量实验表明，URNet在保持高效率的同时取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: URNet通过统一编码器设计有效解决了双编码器架构中的表征不足和计算冗余问题，同时创新的解码器设计填补了该领域对解码器探索的不足，实现了性能与效率的平衡。

Abstract: Previous RGB-D semantic segmentation methods commonly employ dual encoders to separately process RGB and depth inputs, followed by dedicated modules for cross-modal feature fusion. However, such designs often inadequately capture depth representations and consequently limit effective cross-modal interaction, while the additional encoder branch introduces redundant computation that hinders lightweight execution. To tackle these challenges, we propose URNet, a Unified Reparameterized RGB-D Network that performs simultaneous multi-modal feature extraction and cross-modal fusion within a single encoder. Specifically, we adopt a reparameterization strategy to compact the network architecture and facilitate fast inference. Within each Reparameterized Block (RepBlock), a Linear Gated Attention (LGA) module is introduced to fully exploit complementary RGB and depth cues across different feature scales. Furthermore, considering that decoder design has been relatively underexplored in existing RGB-D segmentation models, we develop a concise yet effective universal decoder, termed the Pyramid Merging Decoder (PMD). Extensive experiments on multiple RGB-D segmentation benchmarks demonstrate that URNet achieves state-of-the-art performance while maintaining high efficiency. Code will be available at https://github.com/Wild-Stephen/URNet.

</details>


### [36] [DistMedVL: Distributional Vision-Language Alignment for Uncertainty-Aware Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05683)
*Jiaxuan Li,Qing Xu,Xiangjian He,Yue Li,Daokun Zhang,Fiseha B. Tesema,Rong Qu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DistMedVL，一个概率视觉-语言分割框架，通过轻量级概率跨模态适配器（PCM-Adapter）显式建模表示不确定性，在8个医学分割基准上以6.3M可训练参数超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言分割方法依赖确定性跨模态匹配，忽略了来自模糊边界的偶然不确定性和有限训练数据导致的认知不确定性，在领域偏移下表现脆弱。

Method: DistMedVL在冻结编码器上引入轻量级PCM-Adapter，包含两个顺序模块：Mahalanobis对齐模块（MAM）将文本标记建模为高斯分布，通过Mahalanobis距离计算补丁-文本兼容性并降权不可靠特征维度；分布流模块（DFM）估计模态置信参数并进行视觉引导的文本分布细化。

Result: 在8个医学分割基准上，DistMedVL仅用6.3M可训练参数即超越最先进方法，展现出优越的数据效率、扰动鲁棒性和跨数据集泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过概率化建模跨模态表示不确定性，可以有效提升医学图像分割在真实临床条件下的鲁棒性和泛化性能。

Abstract: Cross-modal alignment of visual and textual representations is fundamental to multimodal medical image understanding, yet remains hindered by uncertainty in both modalities under real-world clinical conditions. Existing vision-language segmentation methods rely on deterministic cross-modal matching, which overlooks aleatoric uncertainty from ambiguous boundaries and epistemic uncertainty from limited training data, leading to fragile performance under domain shift. To address this issue, we propose DistMedVL, a probabilistic vision-language framework that introduces a lightweight Probabilistic Cross-Modal Adapter (PCM-Adapter) upon frozen encoders to explicitly model representational uncertainty. Specifically, the PCM-Adapter comprises two sequential modules for progressive probabilistic alignment. We first devise a Mahalanobis Alignment Module (MAM) that models textual tokens as Gaussian distributions and computes patch-text compatibility via Mahalanobis distance, yielding variance-conditioned matching that downweights unreliable feature dimensions. Moreover, we devise a Distribution Flow Module (DFM) that estimates modality-wise confidence parameters and performs vision-guided refinement of textual distributions, accommodating distributional variation across imaging modalities. Extensive experiments across eight medical segmentation benchmarks demonstrate that DistMedVL outperforms state-of-the-art methods with only 6.3M trainable parameters, exhibiting superior data efficiency, perturbation robustness and cross-dataset generalization.

</details>


### [37] [SciQNet: Two-Stage Multimodal Adaptation for Scientific Image Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05691)
*Yin-Loon Khor,Yi-Jie Wong,Jing Jie Tan,Ming Jie Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SciQNet，一个两阶段多模态自适应框架用于科学图像质量评估。第一阶段进行域自适应预训练，第二阶段结合评分与理解监督进行任务特定微调，在ICME 2026科学图像质量评估挑战赛中获评分赛道第二名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学图像与自然图像不同，其质量同时取决于视觉清晰度和科学信息性，传统图像质量评估方法难以直接适用，需要专门针对科学图像的评估框架。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段在科学文档图像上进行域自适应预训练；第二阶段采用联合评分与理解监督进行微调，其中评分监督结合指令调优和基于评分词logits的Huber损失，理解监督采用多项选择视觉问答形式。

Result: 使用40%分层子集的域自适应数据取得最佳性能，SIQA-S得分92.21，SIQA-U得分47.38，综合得分69.80，在ICME 2026挑战赛评分赛道排名第二。

Conclusion: 预训练数据的相关性可能比数据规模更重要，联合评分与理解监督能够有效提升科学图像质量评估的性能。

Abstract: Scientific images are essential for communicating experimental observations, quantitative evidence and conceptual knowledge. Unlike natural images, their quality depends on both visual clarity and scientific informativeness, making assessment challenging. In this work, we present SciQNet, a two-stage multimodal adaptation framework for scientific image quality assessment. The first stage performs domain-adaptive pretraining on scientific document images and the second stage conducts task-specific fine-tuning with joint scoring and understanding supervision. For scoring-oriented supervision, we combine instruction tuning with a Huber loss derived from rating-word logits, while understanding-oriented supervision is formulated as multiple-choice visual question answering. Experiments show that using a 40% stratified subset of the domain-adaptive data gives the best performance among the evaluated pretraining fractions, suggesting that pretraining-data relevance may be as important as pretraining-data scale. The final model achieves an SIQA-S score of 92.21, an SIQA-U score of 47.38 and a combined score of 69.80. This work presents our solution to the ICME 2026 Scientific Image Quality Assessment Challenge, which ranked 2nd in the scoring track.

</details>


### [38] [TAU-Bench: From Anomaly Instance Tracking to Fine-Grained Video Anomaly Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05699)
*Kepeng Yang,Dongxuan Liu,Rongxin Gao,Zixin Su,Rui Wu,Shuzhao Xie,Chenxin Li,Panwang Pan,Yuzhi Huang,Yue Huang,Jingyan Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了TAU-Bench，一个以轨迹为中心的视频异常理解基准，用于联合评估异常实例跟踪和细粒度异常理解，揭示了现有VLM在语义合理性与视觉定位之间存在持续差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频异常理解方法虽然能生成详细合理的异常描述，但其语义流畅性不能保证模型对正确异常实例的持续跟踪；现有基准将跟踪和语义理解分开评估，导致实例-语义不一致问题未被有效度量。

Method: 构建了TAU-Bench基准，包含1,118个视频、1,454条轨迹和202,438个像素级掩码，覆盖49个事件类别和45个场景类别；通过自动数据引擎实现异常适用性过滤、异常实例轨迹构建、层级化字幕标注和人工质量控制。

Result: 对代表性VLM家族的评估显示，模型虽能生成看似合理的异常解释，但在异常实例的定位和可靠跟踪方面表现不佳，暴露出语义推理与视觉定位之间的持续差距。

Conclusion: 以实例为中心（instance-grounded）的评估是迈向更忠实、更可靠视频异常理解系统的重要一步。

Abstract: Humans understand anomalous events through a coherent perceptual process in which they identify the focal instance, follow its behavior as the event unfolds, and interpret why it violates the expectations of the surrounding scene. Video anomaly understanding (VAU) seeks to endow models with a similar capability, moving beyond deciding whether a video is anomalous toward explaining how the event develops and why it matters. Although recent vision--language models (VLMs) can generate detailed and plausible anomaly descriptions, their semantic fluency does not ensure that these interpretations remain grounded in the correct anomaly instance over time. Existing benchmarks typically evaluate tracking and semantic understanding through separate protocols, leaving such instance--semantic inconsistency largely unmeasured. We therefore introduce TAU-Bench, a track-centric benchmark for jointly evaluating anomaly instance tracking and fine-grained anomaly understanding. TAU-Bench contains 1,118 videos, 1,454 tracks, and 202,438 pixel-level masks spanning 49 event and 45 scene categories, together with track-centric annotations that connect instance-level identification, event-level understanding, and scene-level reasoning. To build TAU-Bench at scale, we developed an automated data engine integrating anomaly suitability filtering, anomaly instance track construction, hierarchical caption annotation, and human quality control. Evaluations across representative VLM families show that models producing plausible anomaly interpretations may still fail to localize and track the correct instance reliably, revealing a persistent gap between semantic reasoning and visual grounding. These findings therefore highlight instance-grounded evaluation as an important step toward more faithful and reliable VAU systems.

</details>


### [39] [StreamArena: Toward Continuous, Interactive, and Long-Horizon Agentic Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05703)
*Xichen Zhang,Guankai Li,Yinghao Zhu,Shijian Wang,Sitong Wu,Shaozuo Yu,Meng Chu,Yuan Lu,Jiaya Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出StreamArena基准，用于评估小时级交互式流媒体视频理解，并推出StreamMind双层级架构，在持续交互与长时间多模态记忆之间取得平衡，超越现有流媒体基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估主要依赖短视频片段和多选题形式，导致仅处理最后几帧的简单基线也能匹敌复杂流媒体模型，且选项暴露语言捷径。缺乏小时级、交互式、开放式的流媒体视频理解基准。

Method: 构建StreamArena基准，包含243个全长视频（平均88.8分钟）和3,646个开放式问答对，评估实时感知、历史回顾、主动交互和多模态工具利用四种能力。提出StreamMind双层级架构：前端worker独立调度处理延迟敏感交互和主动监控，后端worker异步构建持久化多模态记忆并执行历史回忆和外部搜索。

Result: 实验揭示了持续交互与长时间多模态理解之间的张力：仅保留近期帧的方法无法恢复遥远的过往事件，将过去观察转化为文本的方法丢失视觉证据，反复压缩视觉记忆的方法难以保留细粒度细节。StreamMind在所有四种能力上超越现有流媒体基线，并通过重用持久化状态降低查询到回答的延迟。

Conclusion: StreamArena为小时级流媒体视频理解提供了更严格的评估基准，StreamMind证明了前后端解耦的双层级架构是解决持续交互与长时记忆之间矛盾的有效方案。

Abstract: Deploying autonomous multimodal agents in continuous, real-world environments requires them to ingest unbounded audio-visual streams and maintain hour-scale memory. However, current evaluations predominantly rely on brief clips and multiple-choice formats. This design allows minimal baselines that process only the last four frames to match or surpass complex streaming models, while answer options also expose language shortcuts. We introduce StreamArena, a benchmark for hour-scale, interactive streaming video understanding. StreamArena contains 243 full-length videos averaging 88.8 minutes and 3,646 rigorously annotated, open-ended question-answer pairs that evaluate real-time perception, historical retrospection, proactive interaction, and multimodal tool utilization. Evaluation across diverse systems exposes a tension between continuous interaction and long-horizon multimodal comprehension. Methods that retain only recent frames cannot recover distant events, methods that convert past observations into text lose visual evidence, and methods that repeatedly compress visual memory struggle to preserve fine-grained details over time. We address this tension with StreamMind, a two-tier architecture that assigns latency-critical interaction and proactive monitoring to independently scheduled frontend workers, while backend workers asynchronously construct persistent multimodal memory and perform historical recall and external search. StreamMind outperforms existing streaming baselines across all four capabilities and reduces query-to-answer latency by reusing persistent state.

</details>

*Puyuan Zhang,Jianming Huang,Wenkai Ye,Wei Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出G²ARD-GS，一种几何引导的蒸馏方法，将稠密的彩色LiDAR 3DGS模型转化为紧凑且可复用的表示，在5倍到30倍压缩预算下实现最优的渲染质量和几何精度保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: G²ARD-GS通过几何引导蒸馏，将稠密高斯先验（训练无关点云提升或已训练GS模型）渐进融合为表面感知的表示，然后在固定拓扑下（不增删图元）结合施工期锚定约束恢复外观。在有限监督下，使用几何感知的视角选择来分配可用视角预算。

Conclusion: G²ARD-GS通过几何引导的蒸馏策略，在强压缩下兼顾了渲染质量和几何保真度，实现了紧凑且可复用的3DGS表示。

Abstract: Dense colored LiDAR maps provide accurate city-scale geometry, but lifting them into 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) retains millions of primitives, making the resulting models costly to store, transmit, render, and adapt. Aggressive primitive reduction alleviates this burden, but can remove the local surface support needed for stable novel-view synthesis and downstream geometric use. We introduce G$^2$ARD-GS, a geometry-guided distillation method that converts a dense Gaussian prior instantiated either as a training-free point-cloud lift or a trained GS model into a compact, reusable representation. G$^2$ARD-GS progressively consolidates the prior into surface-aware representatives, then recovers appearance on the resulting fixed topology under construction-time anchor constraints, with no primitives added or removed during recovery. Under limited supervision, geometry-aware view selection allocates the available view budget. On MatrixCity, G$^2$ARD-GS achieves the best PSNR, SSIM, and LPIPS across matched $5\times$--$30\times$ compression budgets, outperforming PUP by $3.2$--$6.8$,dB in PSNR. When reused as frozen geometry, the compact model improves off-trajectory appearance adaptation by $3.7$--$4.9$,dB over PUP 3D-GS and preserves image-to-model registration accuracy on Cambridge KingsCollege at $30\times$ compression. Project page: https://patrick1159.github.io/gardGS-page/.

</details>


### [41] [LAWM-3D: Learning 3D-Aware Latent Actions from Human Videos for Generalizable Robot World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05706)
*Jiarui Yang,Jiale Zhange,Jiawei Li,Hang Guo,Wen Huang,Jinpeng Wang,Peidong Liu,Shu-Tao Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LAWM-3D，通过多视角不变的动作分词、几何对齐约束和非注入式RGB-D联合重建目标三个紧密耦合的设计，学习3D感知的潜在动作，显著提升世界模型在生成质量、物理一致性和泛化能力上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在动作模型（LAMs）主要依赖单视角输入并在2D像素空间中操作，学术界尚未解决一个关键问题：简单地将多视角视频引入LAM训练能否赋予潜在动作3D感知能力？研究表明答案是否定的，原因在于未来帧外观泄漏、相机间外观差异和视角变化等问题。因此需要专门设计机制来解决这些挑战。

Method: LAWM-3D包含三个紧密耦合的关键设计：(1) 多视角不变统一动作分词方案，用于学习3D感知的潜在动作；(2) 几何对齐约束，将中间编码器特征锚定到预训练的3D基础模型上，显式提供跨视角几何对应关系；(3) 非注入式RGB-D联合重建目标，防止从未来帧外观信息中走捷径，迫使LAM聚焦于具有几何意义的运动线索监督。采用大规模人类视频预训练加机器人微调的两阶段范式。

Result: 大量实验表明，所提出的3D感知潜在动作显著提升了世界模型的性能，在生成质量、物理一致性和泛化能力方面均达到最先进（SOTA）水平。

Conclusion: 简单地将多视角视频引入LAM训练不足以获得3D感知能力，需要针对外观泄漏、跨视角差异等问题设计专门的机制。LAWM-3D通过三点紧密耦合的设计有效地学习3D感知潜在动作，证明了其对于开放世界具身智能中世界模型应用的显著价值。

Abstract: World models enable agents to perform forward rollout and planning without real-world interaction. However, their application in open-world embodied intelligence remains limited by the high cost of action annotations and the heterogeneity of action spaces across platforms. Recently, latent action models (LAMs) have alleviated this bottleneck by learning action representations directly from unlabeled human videos in a self-supervised manner. Nevertheless, most existing LAMs rely on single-view inputs and operate primarily in 2D pixel space, raising a fundamental question: can simply incorporating multi-view videos into LAM training endow the learned latent actions with 3D-aware perception? Our study shows that the answer is negative. The primary reasons lie in future-frame appearance leakage as well as inter-camera appearance discrepancies and viewpoint variations. To address these issues, we propose LAWM-3D, which introduces three tightly coupled key designs: (1) a multi-view invariant unified action tokenization scheme for learning 3D-aware latent actions; (2) a geometric alignment constraint that anchors intermediate encoder features to a pretrained 3D foundation model, thereby explicitly providing cross-view geometric correspondences; and (3) a non-injective RGB-D joint reconstruction objective that prevents shortcut learning from future-frame appearance information, forcing the LAM to focus supervision on motion cues with geometric significance. Importantly, these components are not simply stacked but are tightly coupled through a unified motivation. Built upon a two-stage paradigm of large-scale human video pretraining followed by robot fine-tuning, extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed 3D-aware latent actions significantly improve world model performance, achieving SOTA results in generation quality, physical consistency, and generalization ability.

</details>


### [42] [One Ranking, Any Budget: Matryoshka Evidence-to-Context Frame Selection for Long-Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05707)
*Wang Chen,Yu Chen,Xiang Wang,Shuai Li,Jinfa Huang,Xiawu Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MEC框架，将长视频帧选择建模为Matryoshka排序问题，构建单条优先序列，其前缀可复用于任意帧预算，无需重新运行选择器。MEC通过稀疏索引、稀疏探测和局部缩放以及位置自适应排序，在四个基准和六个帧预算上平均准确率比均匀采样提升3.77个百分点，匹配最先进选择器，并将端到端选择延迟降低47.37%-51.19%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频帧选择对大型多模态模型（LMMs）至关重要，因为帧冗余严重且上下文窗口有限。实际应用中帧预算随下游LMM、推理需求和延迟约束而变化，但现有方法为每个预算单独优化帧子集，预算变化时先前选择的证据可能被替换而非渐进扩充，无法跨预算复用。

Method: 将长视频帧选择形式化为Matryoshka排序问题，构建单一优先序列使小前缀聚焦查询条件证据，更大的前缀保留证据并增加更广泛的时态上下文。提出MEC框架：(1)构建可复用的稀疏视频索引；(2)通过稀疏探测和局部缩放发现候选帧；(3)贪婪构建位置自适应排序，早期位置强调证据，后期位置逐步偏向时间覆盖同时保持视觉多样性。

Result: 在四个基准和六个帧预算上，MEC相比均匀采样平均准确率提升3.77个百分点，匹配强最先进选择器性能，并将端到端选择延迟降低47.37%-51.19%。

Conclusion: MEC通过单条可截断的优先排序序列实现了跨预算复用的帧选择，免训练框架在保证准确率的同时大幅降低延迟，为长视频LMM应用提供了实用的帧选择方案。

Abstract: Frame selection is essential for applying Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) to long videos due to severe frame redundancy and limited context windows. Since the appropriate frame budget varies with the downstream LMM, reasoning demands, and latency constraints, a practical selector should serve multiple budgets. However, existing methods typically optimize an isolated frame subset for each predefined budget: when the budget changes, previously selected evidence may be replaced rather than progressively augmented. Ranking frames by a fixed score would allow prefix reuse across budgets, but it ignores the distinct roles of different ranking positions. In this paper, we formulate long-video frame selection as a Matryoshka ranking problem: constructing a single priority sequence whose small prefixes concentrate query-conditioned evidence, while progressively larger prefixes preserve this evidence and add broader temporal context. Efficiently constructing such a ranking is itself challenging, as densely sampling long videos and evaluating frame-query relevance incurs substantial overhead. We therefore introduce Matryoshka Evidence-to-Context (MEC) Frame Selection, a training-free framework that builds a reusable sparse video index, discovers candidates through sparse probing and local zooming, and greedily constructs a position-adaptive ranking: early positions emphasize evidence; later positions progressively favor temporal coverage while preserving visual diversity. A single ranking can thus be truncated to any target budget without rerunning the selector. Across four benchmarks and six frame budgets, MEC improves average accuracy over uniform sampling by 3.77 percentage points, matches strong state-of-the-art selectors, and reduces end-to-end selection latency by 47.37-51.19%.

</details>


### [43] [Iterative Hybrid Discrete-Continuous Viewpoint Planning for UAV Photogrammetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05718)
*Alan Grech,Daniel Pisani,Andre Grima,Carl James Debono,Saviour Formosa,Dylan Seychell*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向无人机摄影测量的迭代混合离散-连续视点规划方法，利用代理重建结果来优化相机网络，兼顾局部重建质量与全局图像网络的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统无人机飞行模式未能充分适应场景几何特征，导致局部重建误差；现有方法在覆盖度、重叠度、视差和分辨率等方面的规划不够精细。

Method: 采用迭代混合离散-连续视点规划方法：利用摄影测量启发式（正面性、成像距离、视差、多视角观测数）对采样表面点进行评分；评估完整视点集的可见性、两两重叠和图连通性；在弱观测区域周围生成候选视点，通过聚类CMA-ES优化进行精细化，并移除冗余视点；最终飞行路径融合近距离细节视点与广域模型覆盖视点。

Result: 在三个合成场景上的评估表明，该方法相比已有无人机路径规划方法，在重建精度和完整性两个方面均有所提升。

Conclusion: 所提出的视点规划方法能够有效平衡重建质量与网络鲁棒性，为目标化无人机摄影测量提供了一种更优的路径规划方案。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) photogrammetry requires camera networks that provide sufficient surface coverage, image overlap, parallax, and resolution, yet conventional flight patterns are often poorly adapted to scene geometry resulting in local reconstruction errors. This paper proposes an iterative hybrid discrete-continuous viewpoint planning method for targeted UAV photogrammetry from a proxy reconstruction. The method scores sampled surface points using photogrammetric heuristics based on frontality, imaging distance, parallax, and multi-view observation count, while also evaluating the full viewpoint set in terms of visibility, pairwise overlap, and graph connectivity. Candidate viewpoints are generated around weakly observed regions, refined using clustered Covariance matrix adaptation evolution strategy (CMA-ES) optimisation, and removed when redundant. The final flight path combines close-range detail viewpoints with wider model-coverage viewpoints, balancing local reconstruction quality with global image-network robustness. Evaluation on three synthetic scenes shows that the proposed method improves both reconstruction accuracy and completeness compared with prior UAV path-planning methods.

</details>


### [44] [PhyLatent: Learning Dynamics-Relevant Representations for JEPA World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05720)
*Xi Zeng,Haojie Ren,Ziying Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了PhyLatent，一种面向JEPA世界模型的动力学相关训练目标，通过三条训练路径解决三种物理状态塌缩失败模式，显著提升了模型预测控制成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者观察到防止全局潜变量塌缩并不能确保表示保留物理状态和行为后果，JEPA世界模型存在三种失败模式：物理不变性塌缩、物理可辨识性塌缩和反事实动力学塌缩。

Method: 通过三条训练路径（物理不变性、物理可辨识性、反事实动力学）来解决三种失败模式，具体实现了物理状态接地、未来表示对齐、静态视觉不变性、反事实分支分离和潜变量去噪。

Result: 在OGBench-Cube上，三种失败率分别从15.60%、6.71%、8.41%降至7.53%、0.95%、4.62%，MPC成功率从70.0%提升至78.1%；在相同架构和规划器下，TwoRooms上成功率从81.0%提升到98.0%，在Reacher和PushT上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明仅全局非塌缩不足以学习可靠的JEPA世界模型状态空间，需要显式的动力学相关训练目标。

Abstract: We propose PhyLatent, a dynamics-relevant training objective for JointEmbedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) world models. Our key observation is that preventing global latent collapse does not ensure that a representation preserves physical states and action consequences. We identify three failure modes in JEPA world models: physical invariance collapse, physical identifiability collapse, and counterfactual dynamics collapse. PhyLatent addresses them through three training pathways: physical invariance, physical identifiability, and counterfactual dynamics, implemented with physical state grounding, future representation alignment, static visual invariance, counterfactual branch separation, and latent denoising. On OGBench-Cube, PhyLatent reduces the three failure rates from 15.60%, 6.71%, and 8.41% to 7.53%, 0.95%, and 4.62%, respectively, and improves model predictive control (MPC) success from 70.0% to 78.1%. With the same architecture and planner, it further improves success from 81.0% to 98.0% on TwoRooms and remains competitive on Reacher and PushT. These results show that global non-collapse alone is insufficient for learning a reliable JEPA worldmodel state space.

</details>


### [45] [Engram-E2VID: Reference-Based Event-to-Video Reconstruction via Generative Activation of Appearance Engrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05728)
*Feiyu Ji,Xiang Li,Hao Ma,Tianxiang Huang,Qingxin Lu,Mengqi Ji,Lei Han,Xiaokang Yang,Xiaoyun Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Engram-E2VID，一种基于结构引导的事件到视频重建框架，通过将参考帧编码为token空间的外观印记（appearance engrams），并利用事件流生成目标时刻的运动-结构支架，在一阶扩散骨干中逐步激活相关外观印记，实现无需像素级对应的重建，在三个基准上PSNR提升最高3.29 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件流仅编码稀疏和异步的对数强度变化，缺乏绝对外观信息，使得目标帧重建具有内在挑战性。核心困难在于将事件派生的目标时刻结构与参考帧相关外观信息有效关联，尤其是在复杂运动和长时间间隔条件下。

Method: 将参考帧编码为token空间的外观印记（appearance engrams），事件流与参考上下文转换为捕获运动边界和目标时刻结构变化的结构支架（motion-structure scaffold）。在一阶扩散骨干中，支架派生的结构token逐层与相关外观印记交互激活，实现token空间内的结构-外观关联，避免直接像素级对应，扩散先验用于补充不确定或新暴露区域。

Result: 在三个基准上，Engram-E2VID相比最强同输入基线PSNR最高提升3.29 dB，LPIPS最多降低0.08，并且随重建间隔增大性能退化更慢。

Conclusion: 所提出的结构引导框架有效解决了事件到视频重建中结构-外观关联的难题，通过token空间关联和扩散先验实现了更鲁棒、更稳定的重建性能。

Abstract: Reference-based event-to-video reconstruction aims to recover target RGB frames from a reference frame and the event stream captured over the reference-to-target interval. Although events provide fine-grained temporal cues, they encode sparse and asynchronous log-intensity changes rather than absolute appearance, making faithful reconstruction intrinsically challenging.
